\texttt{Going Right to (0, 1) leads to an empty cell, but it's also far.} \\
\texttt{However, looking at the board, there are many 'H's blocking the path.} \\
\texttt{Let's re{-}evaluate the board state.} \\

\texttt{Row 4: Empty, Empty, H, Empty, G} \\

\texttt{Wait, I missed something. The goal is at (4, 4).} \\
\texttt{From (0, 0), if I go Right to (0, 1), then Down to (1, 1), then Down to (2, 1), then Down to (3, 1) {-}> **H** (Trap!).} \\

\texttt{If I go Right to (0, 1), then Right to (0, 2) {-}> **H** (Trap!).} \\
\texttt{So I cannot go right column 2.} \\

\texttt{If I go {[}down{]} to (1, 0):} \\
\texttt{Next step: {[}right{]} to (1, 1) is safe. {[}down{]} to (2, 0) is safe.} \\

\texttt{You are at (0, 1).} \\
\texttt{{-} Right to (0, 2) is H.} \\
\texttt{{-} Down to (1, 1) is safe.} \\
\texttt{{-} Left to (0, 0) is P.} \\
\texttt{{-} Up is wall.} \\
\\ 
\texttt{If I go {[}down{]} to (1, 1):} \\
\texttt{Next: {[}down{]} to (2, 1) is safe.} \\
\texttt{Next: {[}down{]} to (3, 1) is H.} \\
\texttt{So I must go {[}right{]} from (1, 1) to (1, 2) or {[}left{]} to (1, 0).} \\
\texttt{If {[}right{]} to (1, 2):} \\
\texttt{Next: {[}right{]} to (1, 3) is safe.} \\
\texttt{Next: {[}right{]} to (1, 4) is safe.} \\
\texttt{Next: {[}down{]} to (2, 4) is safe.} \\
\texttt{Next: {[}down{]} to (3, 4) is H.} \\
\texttt{Next: {[}down{]} to (4, 4) is G!} \\
\\ 
\texttt{This looks like a valid path.} \\
\texttt{Path: (0,0) {-}> (0,1) {-}> (1,1) {-}> (1,2) {-}> (1,3) {-}> (1,4) {-}> (2,4) {-}> (2,3) {-}> (2,2) {-}> (2,1) {-}> (1,1) ... wait, I need to get to (4,4).} \\
\texttt{Let's trace carefully:} \\
\texttt{Start: (0,0)} \\
\texttt{1. {[}right{]} {-}> (0,1)} \\
\texttt{2. {[}down{]} {-}> (1,1)} \\

\texttt{4. {[}right{]} {-}> (1,3)} \\
\texttt{5. {[}right{]} {-}> (1,4)} \\
\texttt{6. {[}down{]} {-}> (2,4)} \\
\texttt{7. {[}down{]} {-}> (3,4) {-}> **H** (Trap!)} \\
\\ 
\texttt{Ah, (3,4) is H. So I cannot go down from (2,4).} \\
\texttt{From (2,4), I can go {[}left{]} to (2,3) or {[}up{]} to (1,4).} \\
\texttt{If {[}left{]} to (2,3):} \\
\texttt{Next: {[}down{]} to (3,3) is safe.} \\
\texttt{Next: {[}down{]} to (4,3) is safe.} \\
\texttt{Next: {[}right{]} to (4,4) is G!} \\
\\ 
\texttt{So the path is:} \\
\texttt{(0,0) {-}> (0,1) {-}> (1,1) {-}> (1,2) {-}> (1,3) {-}> (1,4) {-}> (2,4) {-}> (2,3) {-}> (3,3) {-}> (4,3) {-}> (4,4).} \\
